AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='The project compiles successfully (all three debug flavors produce APKs).\n\n## Diagnosis\n\nThe defect was in `DocumentEditFragment`:\n\n```java\n@Override\npublic void onResume() {\n    super.onResume();\n    checkReloadDisk();\n    _hlEditor.setText(_document.getContent());   // <-- resets selection to 0\n}\n```\n\nEvery time the fragment resumed (returning from another app, from a dialog such as "insert image", etc.), the editor\'s text was unconditionally re-set. `EditText.setText()` replaces the `Editable` and drops the selection, so the cursor jumped back to position 0 — which also explains why an image inserted after switching back landed at the beginning of the file. The call was redundant: the editor content was already up to date, and `checkReloadDisk()` → `loadDocumentIntoUi()` already handles the case where the file changed on disk.\n\nA secondary defect was in `loadDocumentIntoUi()`, which clamped the restored position with `_hlEditor.length() - 1` (off-by-one, and `-1` for an empty document) and had no way to recover a cursor position after the view/activity was destroyed and recreated.\n\n## Fix\n\nIn `app/src/main/java/net/gsantner/markor/activity/DocumentEditFragment.java`:\n\n1. **Removed the cursor-destroying `setText` from `onResume()`** and instead restore the remembered cursor position there.\n2. **Added `_lastEditorCursorPosition`** plus two helpers:\n   - `rememberEditorCursorPosition()` — captures `getSelectionStart()` before the fragment goes away (called in `onPause()`, `onSaveInstanceState()` and when the fragment becomes invisible in MainActivity\'s pager).\n   - `setEditorCursorPosition(int)` — clamps the position into `[0, length]` (fixing the off-by-one) and applies it.\n3. **Persisted the position across recreation** via `SAVESTATE_CURSOR_POS` in `onSaveInstanceState()`/`onViewCreated()`, so rotation or a process restart also restores the cursor.\n4. **`loadDocumentIntoUi()`** now falls back to the remembered position when the editor has no meaningful selection yet (e.g. freshly created view), while still preserving the live position for undo/redo and disk reloads.\n\nExisting behavior (save-on-pause, disk reload checks, undo/redo, formatting) is unchanged.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 892, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 35000, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 892, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 1, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfHqTpjtER8GK9DAnW2Xa', stop_reason=None, session_id='b840da6e-91bd-4fab-926c-1fafdac45f6c', uuid='1d754782-8057-411c-a8d0-46cd3ca47845')
